# Starter guide comparison with downloaded RXP Forever guides

Reviewed October 16, 2026 against Forever GuideMate 0.5.2 and the local `RXPGuides-2` download. This report supports the proposed 0.6.0 starter rework. No starter route changes have been applied as part of this comparison.

## Findings that affect the rework

The downloaded RXP routes are genuinely Forever-aware, but they are not an authoritative replacement for our routes. They include new quests and different batching, still retain some cross-starter detours removed in 0.5.0, and omit several chains we already maintain. Quest coverage must be checked by ID, not by title. Repeated titles such as Secure the Mountain or Stalk With The Earthmother can refer to different quests.

Start the route review with the Human Northshire batching, the Durotar Wayward Weapons placement, and the Night Elf antidote timing. Preserve verified local additions and intentional detour cuts. Do not restore removed quests merely to match RXP. The Skyborne Shaman class-quest ordering needs a separate prerequisite check before choosing either order.

## Where the later RXP chapters are

The download has no single file named 1–35. `Guides/GuideList-forever.xml:4` explicitly loads `Guides/forever/RestedXP-Skyborne_01.lua`. Despite its name, that file contains many Alliance and Horde mainland guides through 60. The ordinary starters and 11–20/12–22 files provide the earlier segments. Presence in this file establishes that the chapters were downloaded, not that every later quest is Forever-specific or tested. Several blocks also carry Classic/TBC-related tags.

## Comparison rules

- The primary comparison pairs our seven loaded starter chapters with the ordinary RXP speedrun starter files. Separate Mage and dungeon routes are checked for apparent omissions and identified where relevant; their full order is outside the primary comparison.
- Our side uses the registered Lua goals after class-action expansion. Coverage means an explicit accept or turn-in action, not merely a prerequisite, objective mention, item instruction, or available database record.
- Counts are the union across race, faction, class, optional, and other branches. They are not the quest count for one character. RXP can include follow-on zones within a starter file. An entry missing from one starter file may occur later or in another guide.
- RXP text is inspected without executing its addon. Its step filters, sticky objectives, completewith directives, optional conditions, and runtime routing are not simulated. Source order is a review aid, not proof of the exact order a player experiences.
- The shared-quest tables compare first explicit accepts and turn-ins. Ranks are computed separately for each action, using only quests shared by the paired files. Different ranks expose source-order changes. They do not compare every kill, vendor, flight, or objective substep.
- Cross-file references below distinguish quests that occur elsewhere from quests absent from the downloaded Forever tree. They do not prove that a quest is reachable, offered to the same class, or intentionally omitted.

## Tauren / Mulgore

Both begin with the Narache introduction. We turn in Rites of the Earthmother (763) before A Task Unfinished (1656); RXP reverses them. We finish Swoop Hunting (761) before Winterhoof Cleansing (754); RXP's first listed Swoop turn-in is later. RXP moves Sparring Match (99108) ahead of Poison Water (748), while ours follows Thunderhorn Totem (756). RXP's file extends into Barrens work. Our Traditions of the Bluff (97485), Ceasing Operations (98427), and Thunderhorn's Report (98435) have no accept/turn-in command anywhere in this downloaded Forever tree. Stalk With The Earthmother 76160/76240 appears in RXP; these are distinct from our retained mining-charge quest, and the previous removal decision should be preserved until deliberately revisited.

Pair: `Guides/Leveling/mulgore.lua` and `Horde-1-12_Mulgore.lua`.

## Orc and Troll / Durotar

RXP turns in Wayward Weapons (97279) immediately after the opening Sarkoth/Cutting Teeth work; ours delays it until after the cave quests. RXP turns in Thazz'ril's Pick (6394) before Burning Blade Medallion (794); ours reverses them. RXP finishes Thwarting Kolkar Aggression (786) and Lost in the Shadows (99123) before A Solvent Spirit (818); ours turns in A Solvent Spirit first. RXP still has a 10–12 Tirisfal chapter for selected classes; our general Tirisfal detour was removed in 0.5.0. Threat from Below 99051/99052 is present in ours and absent from RXP's downloaded tree. RXP's Flawed Power Stone (926) is not a missing mechanic in ours: our Demon Seed instructions already include taking and using the stone.

Pair: `Guides/Leveling/durotar.lua` and `Horde-01-12_Durotar.lua`.

## Undead / Tirisfal Glades

RXP lists Rattling the Rattlecages (3901) before The Damned (376); ours reverses them. RXP turns in Sticks and Bones (86784) before the camping introduction (96656/96607); ours does camping and Vital Intelligence first. RXP places Gordo's Task (5481) before A Putrid Task (404); ours does the reverse. Ours completes The Prodigal Lich beyond the initial 405 turn-in through 357, 366, 409, and 411; the downloaded RXP tree has no accept/turn-in for those four follow-ups. Its large apparent surplus includes a full 12–14 Silverpine chapter and class/Ragefire Chasm preparation, so it is not 49 missing Tirisfal quests.

Pair: `Guides/Leveling/tirisfal-glades.lua` and `Horde-01-14_Undead.lua`.

## Human / Elwynn Forest

RXP weaves Nibbled-On Book (91741) and Book Inventory (92124) into Northshire before Wolves Across the Border/Kobold Camp Cleanup turn-ins. Ours completes much of the old Northshire sequence before those new quests. RXP reaches the camping introduction before Report to Goldshire (54); ours turns in Report to Goldshire first. Ours includes Bounty on Murlocs (46), the Delicate Instruments/Enchanting Supplies work (91723–91725), Good Steel (91732), and An Apple Treat (91738), absent from this downloaded RXP tree. RXP's Human file includes a Dun Morogh outing and an 11–13 Loch Modan chapter; our file retains Loch Modan but removed the general Dun Morogh detour.

Pair: `Guides/Leveling/elwynn-forest.lua` and `Alliance-1-13_Human.lua`.

## Dwarf and Gnome / Dun Morogh

Ours keeps A New Threat (170) and A Refugee's Quandary (3361); RXP's ordinary Dwarf/Gnome file omits them. A New Threat does appear in its separate Mage guides, so it is not absent from RXP overall. We turn in Scalding Mornbrew Delivery/Bring Back the Mug (3364/3365) during the early Coldridge sequence; RXP has class-filtered placements interleaved differently. RXP lists Secure the Mountain 98322 before 98319, while ours lists 98319 before 98322. Ours handles Ammo for Rumbleshot (5541) and Beer Basted Boar Ribs (384) earlier relative to much of the western outing. RXP's file includes an 11–12 Elwynn detour and later Westfall/Loch Modan work. Our Finding Warmth/Rime's Wrath/Treacherous Cold block (99159–99162) has no accept/turn-in in the downloaded RXP tree.

Pair: `Guides/Leveling/dun-morogh.lua` and `Alliance-1-14_DwarfGnome.lua`.

## Night Elf / Teldrassil

RXP lists A Good Friend (4495) and The Woodland Protector (458) before the first Balance of Nature turn-in (456); ours turns in 456 first. RXP prioritizes Iverron's Antidote (3521/3522) earlier around the spider-cave work and lists the second Balance of Nature (457) later. Ours finishes Soft Saber Pelts (87288) before Dolanaar Delivery; RXP turns it in later, after the first Denalan/Zenn work. Ours includes Relics of Wakening/Ursal (483/486), Sleeping Druid/Druid of the Claw (2541/2561), and Escaping Ban'ethil (99053); none has an accept/turn-in in the downloaded RXP tree. Much of our remaining surplus is the beginning of Darkshore or class travel, which RXP puts in other files.

Pair: `Guides/Leveling/teldrassil.lua` and `Alliance-1-10_NightElf.lua`.

## Skyborne / Zephras Isle

The opening Coming of Age through Falling With Style turn-ins match. For Horde Shamans, RXP lists Call of Earth (92466/92467) before Harvesting Windstones (93552); ours places those class-quest steps after The Gift of Skysight (92598). Ours lists The Gift of Skysight before Return to Rorian (92469) and Aetheen of the Gales (92471); RXP lists it after them. This needs a prerequisite and faction/class review, not automatic adoption of either sequence. Ours contains late island conclusions including 92646, 93835, 93836, and 94369 that have no accept/turn-in in RXP's downloaded tree. RXP includes many optional profession camping lessons and mainland handoffs. Its file also contains a main island guide and separate 1–9/9–13 variants, so its raw union must not be treated as one character's itinerary.

Pair: `Guides/Leveling/zephras-isle.lua` and `RestedXP-Skyborne.lua`.
